# Seed 61 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - seed 60's first replicate: its ends where seed 60's are (Iteration 425)

`seed 61 tag2 11h widths noevolved` at ed674ec37 (Iteration 406): seed 60's setup again - no design from an earlier run's
record, the tag-2 requirement, both widths - with no comparison step. The remote checks started 16:31:56, the evolution
16:37:34; it evolved 10 h 17 min (seed 60: 8 h 50). 37,813 designs, generations 0-151: 29,930 alive, 7,639 screened
(20.8%), 244 refused at the reach limit.

**No verdict** - no comparison step; seed 63's compare-fronts places it. **Its ends, each run's own measures, beside
seed 60's:** fastest 3cee75bc70 0.110 (seed 60: 5ee5c38165 0.111), smallest 37d5da9011 15,907 bytes (seed 60:
d3d56a90e2 15,930) - the replicate lands where seed 60 did, at both ends. At 32 bits its fastest ff5f34d84c 0.344
(seed 60: 0.325). Its Q-best 2a2b350006 reads Q 0.188 of s6's, under seed 57's 0.209 - but s6's own figures move with
the code between runs; by the raw numbers (re-measured speed x binary + image x memory: 0.137 x 30,560 x 164 KB against
e6b5f1749e's 0.153 x 27,944 x 160) the two are equal within 0.4%.

**Facts.** Its front: 154 designs, all tag-2; tos on 125, the JIT on 72, tail and msc on none; bss and lean on every
design, thinhdr on 153; the loop compiler on 135 (rtloop 1: 65, 2: 70), rtimm on 10. heldout (sieve): Spearman 0.951,
the fastest third 0.896, the fastest tenth 0.507; the pick's regret +3.0%. Fast-end optimism (review-checks.py curse):
the fastest tenth re-measured 1.095 of its in-run speed (seed 60: 1.066, seed 58: 1.112). Diversity 0.486 at the end;
the smallest still gaining (its last gain at generation 142 of 151, three in the last third, 40 bytes), the best
speed's last 2% at 75. At 32 bits 1,780 died on the corpus - all bfind without tos - and 368 did not compile - all
tail: GCC 181, clang 172, clang with preserve_none 15 - the two classes the after-seed-62 batch fixes; 136 of the front
have a 32-bit speed. Its chain's remote checks, of seed 60's front: results/xcheck-seed61.md.

---
# Evolved VM designs

30174 designs evaluated, 29930 alive, 7639 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 2a2b350006, speed 0.126, binary + image 30,560, memory 164 KB - Q 0.188 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
